1. `Bxe3`: White plays `Rxe3`. Now the Bishop at g5 is gone.

If `e1e3`, Black's options:
1. `Bxe3`: White plays `Bxe3`. Now the Bishop at g5 is gone.
This also removes the attacker, but trades a Rook for a Bishop.

So `d4e3` (trading Bishop for Bishop) is better than `e1e3` (trading Rook for Bishop).
And `d4e3` is likely better than `c1b1` because it removes the attacking Bishop at g5.

The legal moves are `c1b1, d4e3, e1e3`.
I will choose `d4e3`.
